## 💡 HOW TO USE THIS INDEX

### During Chat/Discussion
When discussing a specific screen, say:
- "Show me Screen #X from SCREENS section"
- "Reference Image 376 (Paywall)"
- "What UI elements are in [Screen Name]?"

### Copy-Paste URLs
All image URLs are clickable. Use them to:
- Reference designs in Figma
- Share with team
- Create design specs
- Build mockups

### Screen Flow Examples
Each screen connects to others:
1. Welcome → Account Setup
2. Account Setup → Home
3. Home → Collections
4. Collections → Subscription Paywall
5. Paywall → Checkout

## 📥 DOWNLOADING & STORING ASSETS

### Option 1: Use URLs Directly
All images are public Mobbin URLs - use them directly in:
- Design tools (Figma, Adobe XD)
- Documentation
- Chat/Slack
- Presentations

### Option 2: Save for Offline
```bash
# Download all images locally
mkdir -p ~/fabulous_assets
cd ~/fabulous_assets
# Then copy/paste URLs from above section and download each
```

### Option 3: Create Archive
```bash
# After downloading, create zip for sharing
zip -r fabulous_assets.zip ~/fabulous_assets/
```

---

## 🎬 SCREEN FLOW EXAMPLES

### Example 1: Onboarding Flow
1. **Welcome Screen** - Value prop + CTA
2. **Login/Signup** - Email/password or social
3. **Account Setup** - Name, goal selection
4. **Home Dashboard** - First habit view

### Example 2: Monetization Flow
1. **Home Dashboard** - User uses app for 3-7 days
2. **Paywall Trigger** - Feature gating or time-based
3. **Subscription Screen** - Pricing options
4. **Checkout** - Payment processing
5. **Premium Home** - Unlocked features

### Example 3: Daily Habit Flow
1. **Home Dashboard** - See today's habits
2. **Habit Detail** - Instructions (optional)
3. **Mark Complete** - Checkbox/swipe
4. **Celebration** - Confetti animation
5. **Updated Dashboard** - Streak increments

### Example 4: Collection/Routine Flow
1. **Collections Tab** - Browse featured routines
2. **Routine Detail** - Preview habits
3. **Add Routine** - Confirm selection
4. **Home Dashboard** - New habits added
5. **Track Daily** - Complete habits

---

## 🎨 DESIGN REFERENCES

### Colors (Extracted from UI)
- Primary CTA: Vibrant teal/green (likely #1ABC9C or #0EBD8F)
- Accent: Purple/coral for gamification
- Neutral: Light gray/white for backgrounds
- Text: Dark gray (#333) for accessibility
- Success: Green (#27AE60) for completion/streaks

### Typography (Inferred)
- Headlines: Bold, sans-serif (Inter, SF Pro Display)
- Body: Regular, 16-18px (readability)
- Captions: 12-14px (secondary info)
- Habit names: Medium weight, slightly larger

### Spacing
- Margins: 12-16px
- Padding: 12-16px on cards
- Button height: 48-56pt
- Safe area padding (iPhone): 16pt sides

## 📋 COMPLETE ELEMENT CHECKLIST

### Screens/Views (by type)
- [ ] Onboarding screens (Welcome, Signup, Setup)
- [ ] Core screens (Home, Dashboard, Collections)
- [ ] Account screens (Profile, Settings, Login)
- [ ] Monetization (Paywall, Checkout)
- [ ] Utility (Wallet/Gamification)

### Common UI Patterns
- [ ] Bottom tab navigation
- [ ] Modal dialogs (paywalls, confirmations)
- [ ] Card-based layouts
- [ ] Form inputs with validation
- [ ] Progress indicators
- [ ] Streak counters
- [ ] Habit checkboxes
- [ ] Image carousels

### Interactive Elements
- [ ] Primary buttons (Subscribe, Continue, Start)
- [ ] Secondary buttons (Skip, Not Now, Learn More)
- [ ] Tertiary buttons (Links, help)
- [ ] Toggle switches
- [ ] Checkboxes and radio buttons
- [ ] Pull-to-refresh
- [ ] Swipe gestures
